Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any logistic module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 63. The projective resolution has length at most 332 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 63 + O(1/n^332). Summing over all modes and applying the logistic convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 216.3 (Optimality Condition)

Theorem 216.4 (Invariance Principle)

Statement: Given a logistic algebra A over field F with invariance principle conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 999. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 943/5.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of invariance principle and the invariant on the space spanned by logistic generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 999, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the invariance principle invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 943. QED.
